# Itora: the committee's reconstruction, the army of the marches, the
# western states and three courses of the war for the west. Focus deltas use
# IVN_order_dynamic; western influence uses ADISCORD_west_influence_power.
# The trust of the frontier marches, the balance of the Committee and the
# Staff and the islands prepare the revolt that follows a failed northern
# expedition; the cabinet's war and the settlement after it are drawn below
# the courses only once the revolt begins. The Marches Legion has its own
# tree in IVN/legion.
focus_tree = {
	id = IVN_focus
	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			tag = IVN
		}
	}
	default = no
	continuous_focus_position = {
		x = 50
		y = 1100
	}

	# ----- Committee -----
	focus = {
		id = IVN_committee_report
		icon = GFX_goal_generic_political_pressure
		x = 14
		y = 0
		cost = 1
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }
		available = {
			has_capitulated = no
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		continue_if_invalid = no
		completion_reward = {
			ADISCORD_reform_state_development = yes
			add_political_power = 50
			custom_effect_tooltip = IVN_marches_initialize_tt
			hidden_effect = { IVN_marches_initialize = yes }
			effect_tooltip = {
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = IVN_order_dummy_idea
					add_idea = IVN_ord_committee_report_idea
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = IVN_ord_stability value = 0.02 }
				IVN_refresh_order = yes
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 50
		}
	}

	# ----- Three courses of the western question -----
	# The outcome of the northern expedition decides which course is open.
	focus = {
		id = IVN_humanist_shield
		icon = GFX_goal_generic_defence
		x = 9
		y = 4
		cost = 4
		prerequisite = { focus = IVN_committee_mandate }
		mutually_exclusive = {
			focus = IVN_emergency_order
			focus = IVN_revanche_of_the_marches
		}
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }
		available = {
			has_capitulated = no
			has_government = humanism
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = IVN_northern_question_settled_tt
				IVN_northern_question_settled = yes
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = IVN_expedition_not_failed_tt
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_failed }
					has_completed_focus = IVN_cw_return_of_the_committee
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		continue_if_invalid = no
		completion_reward = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = IVN_humanist_shield_tt
			effect_tooltip = {
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = IVN_order_dummy_idea
					add_idea = IVN_ord_humanist_shield_idea
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = IVN_ord_core_defence value = 0.05 }
				add_to_variable = { var = IVN_ord_stability value = 0.03 }

			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 40
		}

	# ----- The corridor mandate -----

					}
					IBL = {
						exists = yes
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
		bypass = {
			NOT = {
				IBA = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}

				IBL = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
